[EVENT: SYR110570]
Date: 2023-02-03
Country: Syria | Location: Al Sabkhah
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Al Sabkhah Communal Militia (Syria) [Identity militia]
Actor2: QSD: Syrian Democratic Forces [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: SHAAM; SNHR; SOHR; The Euphrates Post [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 3 February 2023, a group of locals blocked the road and seized a number of QSD fuel trucks while they were headed to Al Hasakeh in Al Sabkhah town in Deir ez Zor countryside in conjunction with an attack by local gunmen armed members of the Al Kabeesa tribe from adjacent villages and towns on QSD military checkpoints in the town, leading to clashes between a QSD patrol and the gunmen. Clashes resulted in the killing of a disabled child by a stray bullet, and injuring three others including two QSD members. Some sources claim that the clashes erupted after QSD attacked a protest demanding improved living conditions and the release of detainees. After the clashes QSD brought military reinforcements to the town and the villages in its countryside and imposed a curfew on the area. 1 fatality.
